	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
	for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
	  var c = ca[i];
	  while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);

<script>
// Initialize the 404 image storage object
window.product404Images = {};
// Define the 404Image paths that are re-used by client-side functions
window.product404Images.largeImage = '/on/demandware.static/Sites-kingsize-Site/-/default/dw4bdc821f/images/noimagefound-pdp-large.jpg';
window.product404Images.tileImage = '/on/demandware.static/Sites-kingsize-Site/-/default/dw8b87db0b/images/noimagefound-medium.jpg';
window.product404Images.tileSwatchImage = '/on/demandware.static/Sites-kingsize-Site/-/default/dwdb19bc17/images/NO-SWATCH-TAB.svg';
window.product404Images.pdpSwatchImage = '/on/demandware.static/Sites-kingsize-Site/-/default/dwdb19bc17/images/NO-SWATCH-TAB.svg';
// This function is used to replace failed product-images (generic / common implementation)
function handleProductImageError(img) {
img.onerror = null;
img.setAttribute('data-orig-src', img.src);
img.src = window.product404Images.largeImage;
}
// This function is used to replace failed product-tile images
function handleTileImageError(img) {
img.onerror = null;
img.setAttribute('data-orig-src', img.src);
img.src = window.product404Images.tileImage;
}
// This function is used to replace failed product-tile swatch images
function handleTileSwatchImageError(img) {
img.onerror = null;
img.setAttribute('data-orig-src', img.src);
img.src = window.product404Images.tileSwatchImage;
}
// This function is used to replace failed pdp-swatch images
function handlePDPSwatchImageError(img) {
// Create a reference to the current image
img.onerror = null;
img.setAttribute('data-orig-src', img.src);
img.src = window.product404Images.pdpSwatchImage;
// Create a reference to the current anchor
var anchor = img.parentElement;
// Set the data-image url property to the 404-image
anchor.setAttribute('data-imgurl', window.product404Images.largeImage);
// Initialize the JSON properties
var lgImgJSON = anchor.getAttribute('data-lgimg');
var lgImg = JSON.parse(lgImgJSON);
// Reset the urls for the missing image
lgImg.hires = window.product404Images.largeImage;
lgImg.url = window.product404Images.largeImage;
// Update the JSON attribute to include the new image paths
anchor.setAttribute('data-lgimg', JSON.stringify(lgImg));
}
// This function is used to replace failed pdp-thumbnail images (that fail to load)
function handlePDPThumbImageError(img) {
if(img.src=== window.product404Images.tileImage){
img.onerror = null;
}
img.setAttribute('data-orig-src', img.src);
img.src = window.product404Images.tileImage;
// Initialize the JSON properties
var lgImgJSON = img.getAttribute('data-lgimg');
var lgImg = JSON.parse(lgImgJSON);
// Reset the urls for the missing image
lgImg.hires = window.product404Images.largeImage;
lgImg.url = window.product404Images.largeImage;
// Update the JSON attribute to include the new image paths
img.setAttribute('data-lgimg', JSON.stringify(lgImg));
}
</script>

<div class="homepage-seo hide-mobile hide-tablet">
<b>BIG AND TALL CLOTHING</b>
<p>King Size has been specializing in big and tall clothing for more than 60 years. As experts in hard-to-find fashion, we have all the basics and essentials you’ll ever need, like tees, jeans, and hoodies. We also stock the specialty items you want, like pants with customizable hems, extra-long silk ties, moisture-wicking materials, extra wide width shoes, and swimwear. Whether you require a 72-inch waist, a 38-inch inseam, or a 9XL shirt, we rise to the occasion by equipping every department with the specialty sizes you can't live without.</p>
<b>WHAT ABOUT UNDERWEAR AND SLEEPWEAR?</b>
<p>Forget trying to hunt down skivvies and pajamas at big box department stores. Instead, browse our easy-to-navigate selection of underwear, robes, thermals, sleepwear, undershirts, slippers, and socks from the comfort of your own home. Designed to help you feel confident in your clothes, our undergarments include special features, like temperature control, roomy leg openings, stretch integration, and the latest in compression technology. Also, upgrade your weekend loungewear with a set of flannel pajamas, a plush bathrobe, and a pair of shearling-lined slippers.</p>
<b>DO YOU CARRY NAME BRANDS?</b>
<p>With a vast selection of the latest men's big and tall garments, we're never lacking in name brand apparel. Our in-house labels, KS Sport, KS Island™, and KS Signature are loved by many, as are the clothes, accessories, and undergarments offered by Boulder Creek®, Liberty Blues®, Hanes®, Wranglers®, Levis®, New Balance®, and more. Plus, we have game day merchandise from the NFL®, NBA®, NHL®, and MLB®, so you can support all your favorite teams in style.</p>
<b>WILL I FIND SUITS AT KING SIZE?</b>
<p>Every man should have at least one suit in his closet, especially because the matching pant/jacket combo is a look that never goes out of style and works in a variety of situations. By stocking complete men's big and tall suit sets, suit separates, sport coats, vests, and dress shirts that offer impeccable tailoring and extended sizes, you’ll be ready for work or special events at a moment’s notice. If you have a formal or black tie event, like a gala, wedding, theater opening, or charity event, coming up, then you’ll benefit from our tuxedo jackets and pants. Complete your formal ensemble with a bow tie or extra-long silk tie and a pair of dress shoes from our accessories shop.</p>

<script type="text/javascript">
function certonaRecommendations(res) {
try {
var scheme;
var productTileUrl = "/on/demandware.store/Sites-kingsize-Site/default/Product-GetOtherPageRecommendationTile?siteid=ks";
for (var i in res.resonance.schemes) {
scheme = res.resonance.schemes[i];
console.log(scheme);
if (scheme.display == 'yes') {
updateRecContainerID(scheme.scheme);
productHitTiles(scheme.items, scheme.scheme,productTileUrl, i == res.resonance.schemes.length-1);
}
}
} catch (e) {
console.log('Certona recommendations failed: '+ e.message);
}
}
function updateRecContainerID(scheme) {
var $appendRecosContr = $('.append-cart-recommendations');
var $carouselContr = $('.carousel-recommendations');
var containerID = $appendRecosContr.attr('id');
if("ks" == "rm"){
$appendRecosContr.attr('id','rmaddtocart1_rr');
}
if(scheme.indexOf('addtocart1_rr') != -1){
$carouselContr = $('.cart-recommendations-tiles');
} else if(scheme.indexOf('confirmation1_rr') != -1){
$carouselContr = $('.confirmation-recommendations-tiles');
}
$carouselContr.attr('id', scheme);
}
function lazyLoadImages($image) {
try {
if (!$image){ return };
$image.unveil();
window.onscroll = throttle(function(){
if(typeof $.fn.unveil !== 'undefined') {
$('img.p-image').unveil(50);
}
}, 300);
} catch (e) {
console.log('unveil failed: ' + e.message);
}
}
function throttle(func, wait) {
var preDate = 0;
var args, context;
return function() {
var context = this;
var args = arguments;
var now = +new Date();
if(now - preDate > wait) {
preDate = now;
func.apply(context, args);
}
}
}
function productHitTiles(productArray,scheme,productTileUrl,doneScheming) {
var items = productArray,
loadedSlick = false,
processed = 0;
var loadingImage = "/on/demandware.static/Sites-kingsize-Site/-/default/dw411f4cc6/images/loading.png";
if(scheme.indexOf('cart1_rr') != -1 || scheme.indexOf('cart2_rr') != -1) {
if(scheme.indexOf('addtocart1_rr') != -1 || scheme.indexOf('emptycart1_rr') != -1) {
$scheme = $('#' + scheme);
} else {
$scheme = $('.cart-recommendations-tiles');
}
} else {
$scheme = $('#' + scheme);
}
if ($scheme.length == 0 && scheme.indexOf('cart1_rr') == -1) {
return;
}
for (var j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {
var item = items[j];
var query = '?siteid=ks&pid=' + item.ID;
try{
item['dw_url'] = productTileUrl ==''? "/on/demandware.store/Sites-fbbrands-Site/default/Product-GetOtherPageRecommendationTile"+query : productTileUrl+'&pid='+item.ID+'&scheme='+scheme;
}catch(e){
console.log(e.message);
}
(function(j) {
$.ajax({
url: item.dw_url,
success: function(data) {
var listHTML = [];
if (data.length === 0) {
return;
}
var $data = $(data);
//lazyload if not in cart1 vertical carousel or confirmation page
if (scheme.indexOf('cart1_rr') == -1 && scheme.indexOf('cart2_rr') == -1 && scheme.indexOf('confirmation1_rr') == -1) {
//update ImgTags With LazyLoad Attrs
if ($data.find('img.p-image').length !== 0) {
var $lazyloadImg = $data.find('img.p-image');
$lazyloadImg.each(function (i, e){
e.dataset.src = e.src;
e.src = loadingImage;
});
}
}
$scheme.append($data);
swatchesHover();
swtachHoverImage();
$(".cart-overlay img").load(function(){
$(".ui-dialog-content:visible").each(function () {
$( this ).dialog("option","position",$(this).dialog("option","position"));
});
if($('.cart-overlay').height() > $( window ).height()){
$('.cart-overlay').addClass('cart-mini-overlay');
}
});
// init lazy load
lazyLoadImages($lazyloadImg);
},
error: function() {},
complete: function() {
processed++;
if((items.length) == processed && !loadedSlick) {
loadedSlick = !loadedSlick;
certonaSlickSlider(items.length);
certonaCartSlickSlider(items.length);
lookbookCertonaSlickSlider(items.length);
initQuickViewButtons();
}
}
});
})(j);
};
}
function initQuickViewButtons() {
function addEvents(self){
// initialize local variables
var imageContainer = $(self).find('.product-image');
var $qvButton = $(imageContainer).find('#quickviewbutton');
if ($qvButton.length === 0) {
$qvButton = $('<a id="quickviewbutton" class="quickview">' + Resources.QUICK_VIEW + '<i class="fa fa-arrows-alt"></i></a>');
}
var $link = $(self).find('.name-link');
var productTypeValue = '';
var engine = '';
$qvButton.data('href', $link.attr('href'));
$qvButton.attr({
'title': $link.attr('title')
}).appendTo(imageContainer);
$qvButton.off('click').on('click', function(e) {
//SC-404 WCAG When closing the Quick Shop dialog, focus should move to the product.
$(this).closest('.name-link').focus();
if ($(this).closest('.product-tile').hasClass('product-set')) {
productTypeValue = 'product-set';
} else {
productTypeValue = 'normal-product';
}
if(typeof (MERCH_UTILS) !== 'undefined') {
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_productFindingMethod","recommendation");
if(utag && typeof (utag_data.product_id) !== 'undefined'){
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_recommendingId",utag_data.product_id.join(":"));
}
if ($(this).parentsUntil(".you-may-like").hasClass("einstien-reommendations")){
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_recommendingEngine","einstein");
engine = 'einstein';
} else{
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_recommendingEngine","certona");
engine = 'certona';
}
if(utag && typeof (utag_data.page_type) !== 'undefined'){
var page = utag_data.page_type;
var location="";
if(page == "pdp"){
location = engine + ":you may also like:pdp:right sidebar";
} else if(page == "cart"){
location = "certona:you may also like:cart:bottom bar";
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_recommendingId","");
} else {
location = "certona:you may also like:"+page;
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_recommendingId","");
}
MERCH_UTILS.setCookie("_recommendingLocation",location);
}
}
e.preventDefault();
window.quickview.show({
url: $(this).data('href'),
source: 'quickview',
productType: productTypeValue,
callback: function() {
//closes QV modal on overlay click
$('.ui-widget-overlay').bind('click', function() {
$('#QuickViewDialog').dialog('close');
});
}
});
// We don't want the quickView to also launch the PDP --
// so return false a pre-empt any link continuation
return false;
});
}
$('body').find('.you-may-like .product-tile, .last-visited .product-tile').on('mouseenter', function() {
addEvents(this);
});
}
function swatchesHover() {
// Show swatches on mouse-enter / enter
$('.product-swatches').on('mouseenter', function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
if ($(this).hasClass('show-color')) { return; }
$(this).addClass('show-color');
});
// Hide swatches on mouse-leave / exit
$('.product-swatches').on('mouseleave', function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
if (!$(this).hasClass('show-color')) { return; }
$(this).removeClass('show-color');
});
// Show swatches on 'click' of the colors link
$('.product-swatches-all-showless').on('click', function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
$(this).closest('.product-swatches').removeClass('show-color');
});
// Hide swatches on 'click' of the close link
$('.product-swatches-all').on('click', function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
$(this).closest('.product-swatches').addClass('show-color');
});
if ($(window).width() > 1024) {
$('.thumb-link').hover(function() {
$(this).find('.p-image-rollover').show().siblings('img').css('display','none');
}, function() {
$(this).find('.p-image-rollover').hide().siblings('img').css('display','inline-block');
});
}
}
function swtachHoverImage() {
$('.p-image-rollover').hover(function() {
$(this).css('cursor', 'pointer');
});
$('.p-image').hover(function() {
$(this).css('cursor', 'pointer');
});
$('.swatch-list').on('mouseleave', function() {
// Restore current thumb images
var $tile = $(this).closest('.product-tile'),
$thumbs = $tile.find('.product-image .thumb-link img'),
current = $thumbs.eq(0).data('current'),
currentHover = $thumbs.eq(1).data('current');
if (current) {
$thumbs.eq(0).attr({
src: current.src,
alt: current.alt,
title: current.title
});
}
if (currentHover) {
$thumbs.eq(1).attr({
src: currentHover.src,
alt: currentHover.alt,
title: currentHover.title
});
}
});
$('.swatch-list .swatch').on('click', function(e) {
e.preventDefault();
// Enable swatch toggling on swatch-clicks for mobile
if (!$(this).closest('.product-swatches').hasClass('show-color')) {
$(this).closest('.product-swatches').addClass('show-color');
}
if ($(this).hasClass('selected')) {
return;
}
var $tile = $(this).closest('.product-tile');
var hrefAddress = $(this).data('href');
$(this).closest('.swatch-list').find('.swatch.selected').removeClass('selected');
$(this).addClass('selected');
$tile.find('name-link').attr('href', hrefAddress);
var $thumbs = $tile.find('.product-image .thumb-link img');
var data = $(this).children('img').filter(':first').data('thumb');
var dataHover = $(this).children('img').filter(':first').data('thumb-hover');
var currentAttrs = {
src: data.src,
alt: data.alt,
title: data.title
};
var currentHoverAttrs = {
src: dataHover.src,
alt: dataHover.alt,
title: dataHover.title
};
$thumbs.eq(0).attr(currentAttrs);
$thumbs.eq(0).data('current', currentAttrs);
$thumbs.eq(1).attr(currentHoverAttrs);
$thumbs.eq(1).data('current', currentHoverAttrs);
// Update all productUrls with the active swatch
updateProductUrls($tile, $(this));
}).on('mouseenter', function() {
// get current thumb details
var $tile = $(this).closest('.product-tile'),
$thumbs = $tile.find('.product-image .thumb-link img'),
data = $(this).children('img').filter(':first').data('thumb'),
dataHover = $(this).children('img').filter(':first').data('thumb-hover'),
current = $thumbs.eq(0).data('current'),
currentHover = $thumbs.eq(1).data('current');
// If this is the first time, then record the current img
if (!current) {
$thumbs.eq(0).data('current', {
src: $thumbs[0].src,
alt: $thumbs[0].alt,
title: $thumbs[0].title
});
}
// Only evaluate this if more than one thumb was found
if ($thumbs.length > 1) {
if (!currentHover) {
$thumbs.eq(1).data('current', {
src: $thumbs[1].src,
alt: $thumbs[1].alt,
title: $thumbs[1].title
});
}
}
// Set the tile image to the values provided on the swatch data attributes
$thumbs.eq(0).attr({
src: data.src,
alt: data.alt,
title: data.title
});
// Only evaluate this if more than one thumb was found
if ($thumbs.length > 1) {
$thumbs.eq(1).attr({
src: dataHover.src,
alt: dataHover.alt,
title: dataHover.title
});
}
});
}
function updateProductUrls($tile, $swatch) {
// Initialize local variables
var $nameLink; // Represents the productName container-element
var $tileImage; // Represents the tile-image container-element
var variantHref; // Represents the productDetail link for the selected variant
// Default the productDetail link for the current swatch
variantHref = $swatch.data('href');
// Create references to the tile-elements we need to update
$nameLink = $tile.find('.name-link');
$tileImage = $tile.find('.product-image');
// Update the related productUrls with the swatchUrl
$nameLink.attr('href', variantHref);
$tileImage.find('.quickview').data('href', variantHref);
$tile.attr('data-monetate-producturl', variantHref);
}
function certonaSlickSlider(totalItems) {
var $recommendations = $('.carousel-recommendations'),
slidesTotal = totalItems,
slideMaxSize = { desktop:5, tablet:3, phabtablet:3, Iphone:2.3,mobile:1.4},
toScroll = 1;
slideMaxSize.desktop > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.desktop = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.tablet > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.tablet = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.phabtablet > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.phabtablet = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.Iphone > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.Iphone = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.mobile > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.mobile = slidesTotal :null;
slidesTotal < 4 ? toScroll = 1:toScroll = 4;
if ($recommendations.hasClass('slick-initialized')) {
$recommendations.slick('unslick');
}
if ($recommendations.find('.product-tile').length > 1 && totalItems > 1) {
var maxItemsShown = totalItems < 5 ? totalItems : 5;
// You can unslick at a given breakpoint now by adding:
// settings: "unslick" instead of a settings object
$recommendations.slick({
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.desktop,
slidesToScroll: slideMaxSize.desktop,
dots: false,
infinite: false,
arrows: true,
responsive: [{
breakpoint: 1024,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.tablet,
slidesToScroll: Math.min(toScroll, slideMaxSize.tablet),
dots: false,
arrows: true
}
},
{
breakpoint: 767,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.phabtablet,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: false
}
},{
breakpoint: 640,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.Iphone ,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: false
}
},
{
breakpoint: 480,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.mobile,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: false
}
}
]
});
}
}
function certonaCartSlickSlider(totalItems) {
// Create a reference to the lastVisited / searchResults display
var $lastVisitedSearchResultItems = $('.cart-right-content .cart-recommendations-tiles,.confirmation-right-content .confirmation-recommendations-tiles'),
$cartOverlayHtml = $('.cart-overlay-html .cart-recommendations-tiles'),
slideMaxSize = { desktop:4, tablet:3, phabtablet:3, Iphone:1.4,mobile:1.4},
slidesTotal = totalItems,
$tiles = $lastVisitedSearchResultItems.find('.product-tile');
slideMaxSize.desktop > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.desktop = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.tablet > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.tablet = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.phabtablet > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.phabtablet = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.Iphone > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.Iphone = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.mobile > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.mobile = slidesTotal :null;
// You can unslick at a given breakpoint now by adding:
// settings: "unslick" instead of a settings object
var currentImages
if($lastVisitedSearchResultItems.length!==0){
currentImages = $lastVisitedSearchResultItems;
}
else if($cartOverlayHtml.length!==0){
currentImages = $cartOverlayHtml;
}
else{
return;
}
var imgs = currentImages.find('.thumb-link img')
var count = imgs.length;
imgs.load(function() {
count--;
if ( $lastVisitedSearchResultItems.length > 0 && $(window).width() > 1023 ) {
$lastVisitedSearchResultItems.find('.product-tile').addClass('product-height');
}
$tiles.syncHeight();
if (!count) {
addSlide();
}
}).filter(function() { return this.complete; }).load();
currentImages.on('init', function(event, slick){
//$(this).parent().addClass('loaded');
});
function addSlide(){
if($lastVisitedSearchResultItems.length!==0 && totalItems > 1){
if ($lastVisitedSearchResultItems.hasClass('slick-initialized')) {
$lastVisitedSearchResultItems.slick('unslick');
}
$lastVisitedSearchResultItems.slick({
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.desktop,
slidesToScroll: 1,
dots: false,
infinite: false,
vertical: true,
verticalSwiping: false,
arrows: true,
responsive: [{
breakpoint: 1024,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.tablet,
slidesToScroll: 1,
dots: false,
vertical: false,
arrows: true
}
},{
breakpoint: 767,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.phabtablet,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: false,
vertical: false
}
},{
breakpoint: 640,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.Iphone,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: false,
vertical: false
}
},
{
breakpoint: 480,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.mobile,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: false,
vertical: false
}
}]
// You can unslick at a given breakpoint now by adding:
// settings: "unslick"
// instead of a settings object
});
}
if($cartOverlayHtml.length!==0 && totalItems > 1){
if ($cartOverlayHtml.hasClass('slick-initialized')) {
$cartOverlayHtml.slick('unslick');
}
$cartOverlayHtml.slick({
slidesToShow: 4,
slidesToScroll: 1,
dots: false,
infinite: false,
arrows: true
// You can unslick at a given breakpoint now by adding:
// settings: "unslick"
// instead of a settings object
});
}
currentImages.parent().addClass('loaded');
}
}
function lookbookCertonaSlickSlider(totalItems) {
var $recommendations = $('.lookbook-carousel-recommendations'),
slidesTotal = totalItems,
slideMaxSize = { desktop:4, tablet:3, phabtablet:3, Iphone:2,mobile:2} ;
slideMaxSize.desktop > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.desktop = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.tablet > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.tablet = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.phabtablet > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.phabtablet = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.Iphone > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.Iphone = slidesTotal :null;
slideMaxSize.mobile > slidesTotal ? slideMaxSize.mobile = slidesTotal :null;
if ($recommendations.hasClass('slick-initialized')) {
$recommendations.slick('unslick');
}
if ($recommendations.find('.product-tile').length > 1&& totalItems >1) {
var maxItemsShown = totalItems < 4 ? totalItems : 4;
// You can unslick at a given breakpoint now by adding:
// settings: "unslick" instead of a settings object
$recommendations.slick({
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.desktop,
slidesToScroll: 1,
dots: false,
infinite: false,
arrows: true,
responsive: [{
breakpoint: 1024,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.tablet,
slidesToScroll: 1,
}
},
{
breakpoint: 767,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.phabtablet,
slidesToScroll: 1,
arrows: true,
}
},{
breakpoint: 640,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.Iphone,
slidesToScroll: 1,
}
},{
breakpoint: 480,
settings: {
slidesToShow: slideMaxSize.mobile,
slidesToScroll: 1,
}
}]
});
}
}

		window.monetateQ.push(["trackData"]);
	}
